Actually here's a subtle thing: React's `setSessions` filter removes the row, but the click handler for the *second* rapid click could re-fire before re-render (same tick). Guard by id fixes exactly that.

不过 commit 2 和 1 都在 App.tsx 里，需要分开 add（git add -p 不可交互；可以用 `git add` 整个文件不行）。可以用 stash 或分开提交方式：先提交 e2e 的改动？e2e 改动依赖新界面。

实际做法：commit 1 包含 api/compare/CompareBar/RevisionBar/App.tsx（含 deletingRef？不行，混了）。要拆开只能用 patch 模式。这里可以接受把 deletingRef 单独提交：先把 App.tsx 之外的先暂存并提交，然后 App.tsx 分两次？但 deletingRef 和 startRun 都在 App.tsx。

替代方案：deletingRef 修复先做（已经做了），可以先前置它——但它和 compare 改动在同一文件同一工作区，只能靠 `git add -p` 交互或 `git stash`。

务实做法：把 deletingRef 修复并入单独一个提交，用 `git diff` 生成补丁精细暂存比较麻烦。或者，顺序调整：现在先把 App.tsx 的 compare 相关改动 + 其他文件提交为 feat；deletingRef 也是这轮点测发现的缺陷，可以做第二个提交（此时 App.tsx 剩下的 diff 只有 deletingRef）——但目前两个改动都在工作区里没提交。

我可以用 `git stash push` 不行。用 patch：`git diff packages/web/src/App.tsx > /tmp/app.patch`，然后手工构造只含 deletingRef 的补丁，`git apply --cached`。这有点重。

更简单：既然 e2e 修复（本轮真缺陷）也可独立提交，我就按：
- commit A: test(tools): e2e 补 C1 两条断言 + 修正读取范围（e2e.mjs 单独文件）→ 但 e2e 依赖新界面存在才能跑，不过 commit 顺序不影响历史事实叙述。

其实合理叙述：commit A = feat(web) 前端全套（含 deletingRef 吗？）——不行，deletingRef 与 compare 无关，混进 feat 会让「这个提交对应哪一次对话」说不清。

我采用 git add -p 的非交互替代：用 `git add` 文件级别，把 App.tsx 的 deletingRef 单独提取。

实际最简单：先用 `git stash` 把 App.tsx compare 部分保留... 算了。用 `git diff` 看 App.tsx 差异，然后：
1) 复制当前 App.tsx 到临时文件（全量）。
2) 用 checkout 恢复 App.tsx 到 HEAD，再只应用 deletingRef 三处编辑，提交 fix。
3) 再把全量版本覆盖回来，提交 feat。

这样两个提交都是真实、干净、可编译的中间态（deletingRef 单独存在时也能编译）。这个方法可靠。

- for each plan: if plan.quote === quote → return plan (first run)
- for each plan: if any afterByStyle value === quote → return the "after plan" variant (a derived plan: same text/quote but verdict solid, answer confident, suggestion null).

That's clean and deterministic. Then:
- `probeText`: for each block, resolve plan by quote, then ask its questions.
- `answerText`: currently `planForQuestion(question)` scans DEMO_PLANS's questions. In run 2 the after-variant would have its own questions; need `planForQuestion` to also scan variant questions. Simplest: give the after-variant its own question set and include variants in the global scan.
- `verdictText`: `firstClaimId(prompt)` + `planForClaimId` → also switch to quote-based.
- `suggestText`: uses claimId + quote → in run 2 C2' verdict is solid → suggest skipped anyway (SUGGESTIBLE excludes solid). Others unchanged, still need suggestions (C3,C4,C5 quotes present, plans found by quote).

```
    } else {

    }
  }
  ...
}
```

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

Now I need to design the second-run demo. The plan: scripted client needs to recognize when the resume has been rewritten (second run) and produce different verdicts.

Key mechanism: In the second run, the resume text contains the rewritten lines. The extract stage will produce claims — but the scripted client `extractText()` always returns the same DEMO_PLANS quotes. Those quotes won't be found in the revised resume (because the lines were rewritten), so they'd all be rejected! That's a critical issue.

Wait — after accepting revisions, the rewritten lines differ. For example C2: '主导引入 Redis 缓存，QPS 从 800 提升到 5000。' → after (normal): '引入 Redis 缓存后，同一接口在预发环境单机压测下 QPS 由 800 提升到 5000（压测工具与口径见附录）'. The quote no longer exists verbatim in the revised resume. So `extractText()` returning old quotes → all rejected → no claims → second run is empty.

So the scripted client must detect the revision state and emit extract output based on what's actually in the resume. The cleanest design: make `extractText(prompt)` inspect the resume text in the prompt (the `## 简历原文` section) and, for each DEMO_PLAN, find either the original quote or the rewritten variant. To find the rewritten variant, we need to know the rewrite outputs per style.

Design: each DEMO_PLAN gets an optional `rewritten` field: the set of after-texts (across styles) that may replace the quote. Then `extractText(prompt)`:
- parse the resume section lines
- for each plan: if prompt contains plan.quote → emit as-is (first run). Else if prompt contains any of plan.rewritten variants → emit a new claim with that variant as quote and text (maybe use a "second" variant of text), and record that this claim is "rewritten".

But claimId mapping: DEMO_PLANS index → Cn. In run two, claims are numbered by extract order. If we keep order, C2 remains the rewritten QPS claim. Good.

Then verdict: `verdictText(prompt)` uses firstClaimId → plan. In second run, for C2 we want 'solid'. So verdict plan needs a `rewrittenVerdict` field: when the claim's quote in the prompt is not the original quote (i.e. it's the rewritten variant), use the rewritten verdict/rationale/evidence.

Answer: `answerText(prompt)` looks up by question text. Questions in second run — probeText emits plan.questions. Let me write helper:

```ts
function variantsOf(plan: DemoClaimPlan): string[] {
  const s = plan.suggestion;
  if (s === null) return [];
  const out: string[] = [s.after];
  for (const v of Object.values(s.afterByStyle)) { if (typeof v === 'string' && v.length > 0) out.push(v); }
  return out;
}
function revisedQuoteIn(plan: DemoClaimPlan, prompt: string): string | null {
  if (prompt.indexOf(plan.quote) >= 0) return null;
  for (const v of variantsOf(plan)) { if (prompt.indexOf(v) >= 0) return v; }
  return null;
}
```

Now, the content design. Let me define for each plan a `fixed` variant used when the (accepted) revision has been applied:

```ts
interface DemoFixedPlan {
  /** 改写之后的主张（人会这么写） */
  text: string;
  /** 依据的引文就是建议里的 after 文本，脚本按它反查（三种档位写法不同，全部列出） */
  answer: { ... }  // 同一批问题，这次答得上
  verdict: { kind: 'solid', confidence, rationale, evidenceQuotes: [<the quote itself>] }
}
```

Evidence quotes: they must anchor. Since we detect which quote variant appears in the resume, we can use that exact string as the evidence quote. So verdictText computes evidenceQuotes = [matchedQuote].

Let me now write the resolution helper:

```ts
/** 一条主张在简历里可能的写法：原文，或采纳改写后的任意一档 */
function quoteVariants(plan: DemoClaimPlan): string[] {
  const out = [plan.quote];
  const s = plan.suggestion;
  if (s !== null) {
    for (const value of Object.values(s.afterByStyle)) if (typeof value === 'string' && value.length > 0) out.push(value);
    out.push(s.after);
  }
  return out;
}

function quoteIn(prompt: string, plan: DemoClaimPlan): { quote: string; fixed: boolean } | null {
  const variants = quoteVariants(plan);
  for (let i = 0; i < variants.length; i++) {
    if (prompt.indexOf(variants[i]) >= 0) return { quote: variants[i], fixed: i > 0 };
  }
  return null;
}
```
